EC2 & VPC

112 resource kinds. Generated from the operator's CRD schemas.

API Group: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1

AMI # GA

AMI is the Schema for managing EC2 Amazon Machine Images.

kubectl get amis · example on GitHub

Example

# AMI — EC2 & VPC
# Full-options example generated from the CRD schema. Optional fields are
# included with sample values; delete what you don't need. Field reference:
# https://konfig-konector.io/docs/ec2.html#ami
apiVersion: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1
kind: AMI
metadata:
  name: example-ami
  namespace: default
spec:
  architecture: x86_64
  blockDeviceMappings:
  - deleteOnTermination: false
    deviceName: example-ami
    encrypted: true
    volumeSize: 2
    volumeType: example
  description: Managed by konfig-konector
  enaSupport: false
  name: example-ami
  providerRef:
    name: example-ami
    region: us-east-1
  rootDeviceName: example-ami
  sriovNetSupport: example
  tags:
    environment: dev
    team: platform
  virtualizationType: hvm

Spec

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
architectureenum—Architecture is the CPU architecture (x86_64 or arm64). Allowed: x86_64, arm64, i386
blockDeviceMappingsarray<object>—BlockDeviceMappings defines the EBS volumes and snapshots to attach.
blockDeviceMappings[].deleteOnTerminationboolean—DeleteOnTermination controls whether to delete on instance termination.
blockDeviceMappings[].deviceNamestringyesDeviceName is the device name (e.g.
blockDeviceMappings[].encryptedboolean—Encrypted enables encryption for this volume.
blockDeviceMappings[].snapshotIdstring—SnapshotID is the snapshot to use for this device.
blockDeviceMappings[].volumeSizeinteger—VolumeSize is the size of the volume in GiB.
blockDeviceMappings[].volumeTypestring—VolumeType is the EBS volume type.
descriptionstring—Description is an optional description.
enaSupportboolean—ENASupport enables enhanced networking with ENA.
kernelIdstring—KernelID is an optional kernel ID (for paravirtual).
namestringyesName is the AMI name.
providerRefobject—ProviderRef selects the AWSProvider (account/region) this resource is reconciled against.
providerRef.namestringyesName of a cluster-scoped AWSProvider.
providerRef.regionstring—Region overrides the provider's region for this resource only.
ramdiskIdstring—RamdiskID is an optional ramdisk ID.
rootDeviceNamestring—RootDeviceName is the root device name (e.g.
sriovNetSupportstring—SRIOVNetSupport enables SR-IOV networking (set to "simple").
tagsmap<string>—Tags are AWS resource tags to apply.
virtualizationTypeenum—VirtualizationType is hvm or paravirtual. Allowed: hvm, paravirtual

Status

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
awsProviderobject—AWSProvider confirms the account and region this resource was reconciled against.
awsProvider.accountIdstring—AccountID the resource lives in.
awsProvider.namestring—Name of the AWSProvider used; empty when the operator's own credentials were used.
awsProvider.regionstring—Region the resource lives in (empty for global services).
conditionsarray<object>—Conditions describe the current state of the resource.
conditions[].lastTransitionTimestringyeslastTransitionTime is the last time the condition transitioned from one status to another.
conditions[].messagestringyesmessage is a human readable message indicating details about the transition.
conditions[].observedGenerationinteger—observedGeneration represents the .metadata.generation that the condition was set based upon.
conditions[].reasonstringyesreason contains a programmatic identifier indicating the reason for the condition's last transition.
conditions[].statusenumyesstatus of the condition, one of True, False, Unknown. Allowed: True, False, Unknown
conditions[].typestringyestype of condition in CamelCase or in foo.example.com/CamelCase.
imageIdstring—ImageID is the AMI ID.
lastSyncTimestring—LastSyncTime is when the resource was last successfully reconciled.
observedGenerationinteger—ObservedGeneration is the most recent .metadata.generation reconciled.
statestring—State is the current state of the AMI (pending, available, failed, etc.).

CapacityReservation # GA

CapacityReservation is the Schema for managing EC2 capacity reservations.

kubectl get capacityreservations · example on GitHub

Example

# CapacityReservation — EC2 & VPC
# Full-options example generated from the CRD schema. Optional fields are
# included with sample values; delete what you don't need. Field reference:
# https://konfig-konector.io/docs/ec2.html#capacityreservation
apiVersion: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1
kind: CapacityReservation
metadata:
  name: example-capacityreservation
  namespace: default
spec:
  availabilityZone: us-east-1a
  endDate: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'
  endDateType: unlimited
  instanceCount: 1
  instancePlatform: example
  instanceType: example
  providerRef:
    name: example-capacityreservation
    region: us-east-1
  tags:
    environment: dev
    team: platform
  tenancy: default

Spec

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
availabilityZonestringyesAvailabilityZone in which to create the reservation.
endDatestring—EndDate is when the reservation expires (required when endDateType is limited).
endDateTypeenum—EndDateType indicates how the reservation ends. Allowed: unlimited, limited
instanceCountintegeryesInstanceCount is the number of instances to reserve capacity for.
instancePlatformstringyesInstancePlatform is the operating system type for the reserved capacity (e.g.
instanceTypestringyesInstanceType for which to reserve capacity.
providerRefobject—ProviderRef selects the AWSProvider (account/region) this resource is reconciled against.
providerRef.namestringyesName of a cluster-scoped AWSProvider.
providerRef.regionstring—Region overrides the provider's region for this resource only.
tagsmap<string>—Tags are AWS resource tags to apply.
tenancyenum—Tenancy of the reservation. Allowed: default, dedicated

Status

FieldTypeRequiredDescription

awsProviderobject—AWSProvider confirms the account and region this resource was reconciled against.
awsProvider.accountIdstring—AccountID the resource lives in.
awsProvider.namestring—Name of the AWSProvider used; empty when the operator's own credentials were used.
awsProvider.regionstring—Region the resource lives in (empty for global services).
capacityReservationIdstring—CapacityReservationID is the AWS capacity reservation identifier.
conditionsarray<object>—Conditions describe the current state of the resource.
conditions[].lastTransitionTimestringyeslastTransitionTime is the last time the condition transitioned from one status to another.
conditions[].messagestringyesmessage is a human readable message indicating details about the transition.
conditions[].observedGenerationinteger—observedGeneration represents the .metadata.generation that the condition was set based upon.
conditions[].reasonstringyesreason contains a programmatic identifier indicating the reason for the condition's last transition.
conditions[].statusenumyesstatus of the condition, one of True, False, Unknown. Allowed: True, False, Unknown
conditions[].typestringyestype of condition in CamelCase or in foo.example.com/CamelCase.
lastSyncTimestring—LastSyncTime is when the resource was last successfully reconciled.
observedGenerationinteger—ObservedGeneration is the most recent .metadata.generation reconciled.
statestring—State is the lifecycle state reported by AWS.

EBSVolume # GA

EBSVolume is the Schema for managing EBS Volumes.

kubectl get ebsvolumes · example on GitHub

Example

# EBSVolume — EC2 & VPC
# Full-options example generated from the CRD schema. Optional fields are
# included with sample values; delete what you don't need. Field reference:
# https://konfig-konector.io/docs/ec2.html#ebsvolume
apiVersion: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1
kind: EBSVolume
metadata:
  name: example-ebsvolume
  namespace: default
spec:
  availabilityZone: us-east-1a
  encrypted: true
  iops: 1
  kmsKeyId: arn:aws:kms:us-east-1:123456789012:key/1234abcd-12ab-34cd-56ef-1234567890ab
  multiAttachEnabled: true
  providerRef:
    name: example-ebsvolume
    region: us-east-1
  size: 1
  tags:
    environment: dev
    team: platform
  throughput: 1
  volumeType: gp2

Spec

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
availabilityZonestringyesAvailabilityZone is the AZ in which to create the volume.
encryptedboolean—Encrypted enables EBS encryption.
iopsinteger—IOPS is the requested IOPS (for io1, io2, gp3).
kmsKeyIdstring—KMSKeyID is the KMS key ID/ARN for encryption.
multiAttachEnabledboolean—MultiAttachEnabled enables Multi-Attach for io1/io2 volumes.
providerRefobject—ProviderRef selects the AWSProvider (account/region) this resource is reconciled against.
providerRef.namestringyesName of a cluster-scoped AWSProvider.
providerRef.regionstring—Region overrides the provider's region for this resource only.
sizeinteger—Size is the volume size in GiB.
snapshotIdstring—SnapshotID creates the volume from an existing snapshot.
tagsmap<string>—Tags are AWS resource tags to apply.
throughputinteger—Throughput is the throughput in MiB/s (for gp3).
volumeTypeenum—VolumeType is the EBS volume type (gp2, gp3, io1, io2, sc1, st1, standard). Allowed: gp2, gp3, io1, io2, sc1, st1, standard

Status

FieldTypeRequiredDescription

Example

# EC2ApplicationStatusCheck — EC2 & VPC
# Full-options example generated from the CRD schema. Optional fields are
# included with sample values; delete what you don't need. Field reference:
# https://konfig-konector.io/docs/ec2.html#ec2applicationstatuscheck
apiVersion: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1
kind: EC2ApplicationStatusCheck
metadata:
  name: example-ec2applicationstatuscheck
  namespace: default
spec:
  aggregation: included
  deviceIndex: 1
  failureThreshold: 1
  initializationGracePeriodSeconds: 1
  interval: 1
  ipScope: private
  ipVersion: ipv4
  path: example
  port: 1
  protocol: http
  providerRef:
    name: example-ec2applicationstatuscheck
    region: us-east-1
  statusCodeMatcher: example
  successThreshold: 1
  tags:
  - key: example
    value: example
  timeout: 300

Spec

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
aggregationenum—Whether this check is included in the rolled-up application status. Allowed: included, excluded
deviceIndexinteger—The network interface device index used for the health check.
failureThresholdinteger—The number of consecutive failed probes required to mark the instance unhealthy.
healthCheckPathsarray<object>—The source/destination network paths used for the health check.
healthCheckPaths[].destinationsarray<object>—
healthCheckPaths[].destinations[].securityGroupIdstring—
healthCheckPaths[].destinations[].subnetIdstring—
healthCheckPaths[].sourceobject—EC2ApplicationStatusCheckHealthCheckPathSource is a nested property type of AWS::EC2::ApplicationStatusCheck.
healthCheckPaths[].source.securityGroupIdstring—
healthCheckPaths[].source.subnetIdstring—
initializationGracePeriodSecondsinteger—Seconds to wait after instance launch before beginning health checks.
intervalinteger—The interval, in seconds, between health check probes.
ipScopeenum—The IP scope used for the health check. Allowed: private
ipVersionenum—The IP version used for the health check. Allowed: ipv4, ipv6
pathstring—The HTTP path used for the health check.
portintegeryesThe port used for the health check.
protocolenumyesThe network protocol used for the health check. Allowed: http, https
providerRefobject—ProviderRef selects the AWSProvider (account/region) this resource is reconciled against.
providerRef.namestringyesName of a cluster-scoped AWSProvider.
providerRef.regionstring—Region overrides the provider's region for this resource only.
statusCodeMatcherstring—The HTTP status codes considered successful (e.g., "200-299").
successThresholdinteger—The number of consecutive successful probes required to mark the instance healthy.
tagsarray<object>—Tags to apply to the application status check.
tags[].keystringyes
tags[].valuestringyes
timeoutinteger—The timeout, in seconds, for each health check probe.

Status

EC2NetworkInsightsPath # CLOUD CONTROL · AWS::EC2::NetworkInsightsPath

EC2NetworkInsightsPath manages AWS::EC2::NetworkInsightsPath through the AWS Cloud Control API.

kubectl get ec2networkinsightspaths · example on GitHub

Example

# EC2NetworkInsightsPath — EC2 & VPC
# Full-options example generated from the CRD schema. Optional fields are
# included with sample values; delete what you don't need. Field reference:
# https://konfig-konector.io/docs/ec2.html#ec2networkinsightspath
apiVersion: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1
kind: EC2NetworkInsightsPath
metadata:
  name: example-ec2networkinsightspath
  namespace: default
spec:
  destination: example
  destinationIp: example
  destinationPort: 443
  filterAtDestination:
    destinationAddress: example
    destinationPortRange:
      fromPort: 443
      toPort: 443
    sourceAddress: example
    sourcePortRange:
      fromPort: 443
      toPort: 443
  filterAtSource:
    destinationAddress: example
    destinationPortRange:
      fromPort: 443
      toPort: 443
    sourceAddress: example
    sourcePortRange:
      fromPort: 443
      toPort: 443
  protocol: tcp
  providerRef:
    name: example-ec2networkinsightspath
    region: us-east-1
  source: example
  sourceIp: example
  tags:
  - key: example
    value: example

awsProvider.namestring—Name of the AWSProvider used; empty when the operator's own credentials were used.
awsProvider.regionstring—Region the resource lives in (empty for global services).
conditionsarray<object>—Conditions describe the current state of the resource.
conditions[].lastTransitionTimestringyeslastTransitionTime is the last time the condition transitioned from one status to another.
conditions[].messagestringyesmessage is a human readable message indicating details about the transition.
conditions[].observedGenerationinteger—observedGeneration represents the .metadata.generation that the condition was set based upon.
conditions[].reasonstringyesreason contains a programmatic identifier indicating the reason for the condition's last transition.
conditions[].statusenumyesstatus of the condition, one of True, False, Unknown. Allowed: True, False, Unknown
conditions[].typestringyestype of condition in CamelCase or in foo.example.com/CamelCase.
lastSyncTimestring—LastSyncTime is when the resource was last successfully reconciled.
observedGenerationinteger—ObservedGeneration is the most recent .metadata.generation reconciled.
statestring—State is the lifecycle state reported by AWS.
vpnGatewayIdstring—VPNGatewayID is the AWS virtual private gateway identifier.
